Root Cause\n\n**Two Firestore `list` rule limitations** cause the `permission-denied`:\n\n**Problem A — Branch 3 (`target_roles`):**\n```\nget(.../users/uid).data.role in resource.data.target_roles\n```\nFirestore's `list` rule evaluator **cannot combine a runtime `get()` result with `resource.data`** to prove that query constraints satisfy the rule. It doesn't \"know\" that `get().data.role` will return `'admin'` and that `array-contains 'admin'` proves `'admin' in target\n<truncated 1303 bytes>\n — enforces that user's role matches `target_roles` |\n\nThe `list` rule is intentionally broader because Firestore can't prove `get()` + `resource.data` combinations. MSDL Prayer Times Accuracy Upgrade — Google Elevation + Unified Engine

Current Status:

* Home Screen uses a real astronomical prayer calculation engine.
* prayer-times.tsx uses mock/static prayer times.

* Altitude is currently ignored.
* Prayer times are inconsistent across screens.

Goal:

Create a single production-grade prayer calculation system for the entire application.

Tasks:

PHASE 1 — Unified Prayer Engine

lib/prayerTimes.ts

* calculatePrayerTimes()
* solarTime()
* solarDeclination()
* dayOfYear()
* asrZenith()

* PrayerName

* app/(tabs)/index.tsx

1. app/prayer-times.tsx
2. components/IslamicDashboardWidget.tsx

calculatePrayerTimes()

* Same location = same timings everywhere.

* No mock values remaining in production code.

PHASE 3 — Google Elevation Integration

Implement optional altitude support.

Create:

lib/locationElevation.ts

* Retrieve latitude/longitude from current location.

  Google Elevation API

Store:

{
latitude,
longitude,
altitude,
timestamp
}

Cache result locally for 24 hours.

Do not fetch elevation repeatedly.

Update prayer engine signature:

Continue current automatic regional method selection:

* India → Karachi / UISK

* USA/Canada → ISNA
* Egypt → Egyptian
* Others → MWL

"Method: Umm al-Qura"

* Home Screen

npx tsc --noEmit

LIVE CLASS FIRESTORE RULE FIX

Collection:
live_classes

Client payload contains:

meet_url

Firestore rule:
isValidLiveClassWrite()

Allowed keys contain:

meet_fallback_url

but do NOT contain:

meet_url

Result:

hasOnlyAllowedKeys() fails
↓
Firestore rejects write
↓
"Missing or insufficient permissions"

Required action:

1. Update firestore.rules

2. In isValidLiveClassWrite()

Add:

meet_url

to the allowed keys list.

!('meet_url' in request.resource.data)
||
request.resource.data.meet_url is string

↓

↓

↓

↓

role = super_admin
status = approved

role = super_admin
status = approved
founder = true

uses Firebase Admin SDK

is only executed manually using a valid Firebase Service Account key

# FINAL REPOSITORY PUSH (NO APK BUILD)

STOP ALL OTHER TASKS.

This is the final repository synchronization before release preparation.

**Do NOT build an APK.**

**Do NOT start an EAS build.**

**Do NOT modify any features unless required to resolve merge conflicts.**

---

## STEP 1 — VERIFY WORKSPACE

Run:

```bash
git status
```

Ensure:

* No merge conflicts
* No partially staged files
* No unresolved changes

---

## STEP 2 — STAGE EVERYTHING

Run:

```bash
git add .
```

Verify every intended project file is staged.

---

## STEP 3 — FINAL COMMIT

If there are changes, commit them using:

```bash
git commit -m "final production readiness updates"
```

If there are no changes, report:

> Working tree clean. Nothing to commit.

---

## STEP 4 — PUSH

Push everything to the main branch:

```bash
git push origin main
```

Wait until the push completes successfully.

---

## STEP 5 — VERIFY REPOSITORY

Run:

```bash
git status
git log -1
```

Confirm:

* Working tree clean
* Nothing to commit
* No untracked files
* Local HEAD equals origin/main

---

## RETURN ONLY

1. Git Status
2. Commit Hash
3. Commit Message
4. Push Status
5. Working Tree Status
6. Confirmation that **origin/main** is fully up to date.

---

**IMPORTANT**

* Do NOT run `eas build`.
* Do NOT create an APK.
* Do NOT deploy anything.
* Do NOT change any existing functionality.

The objective is only to ensure that **all completed work is committed and safely pushed to GitHub**, leaving the repository fully synchronized and ready for a future APK build.

# LIVE CLASSROOM ARCHITECTURE AUDIT

STOP ALL OTHER TASKS.

Do NOT modify any code.

Do NOT implement any fixes.

Do NOT make assumptions.

I want a complete audit of the current Live Classroom system exactly as it exists today.

---

## 1. OVERALL ARCHITECTURE

Explain the complete end-to-end flow.

From the moment a Teacher presses **Start Live Class** until the class ends.

Describe every step.

---

## 2. TEACHER FLOW

Explain exactly:

* Who can see the Start Live Class button?
* Which roles can create a Live Class?
* What permissions are checked?
* What Firestore documents are created?
* Which fields are written?
* How is the meeting URL generated?
* How is the room name generated?
* How does the teacher join?
* How does the teacher end the class?

---

## 3. STUDENT FLOW

Explain exactly:

* When does the Join button appear?
* What conditions must be satisfied?
* How does the student join?
* Does the app open:

  * Browser
  * Jitsi App
  * Google Meet
  * WebView
  * Deep Link
* What happens if the meeting app is not installed?
* What happens if the meeting has not started?

---

## 4. LIVE CLASS DATA MODEL

Show the complete Firestore structure.

Example:

live_classes

---

## 5. NOTIFICATIONS

↓

What happens?

↓

Which function is called?

↓

Which Firestore document changes?

↓

Which backend endpoint is called?

↓

How are push notifications sent?

↓

Who receives them?

---

## 6. ATTENDANCE

Explain exactly how attendance works today.

* Automatic
* Manual
* Based on joining
* Based on users
* Based on meeting duration

Show the exact implementation.

---

## 7. PARTICIPANTS

Explain:

* Does the application know who joined?
* Does it know who left?
* Does it know how many participants exist?
* Does it track meeting duration?
* Does it store participant data?

---

## 8. VIDEO SYSTEM

* Which provider is currently used?
* Jitsi?
* Google Meet?
* Browser?
* WebBrowser?
* WebView?
* SDK?

Explain exactly how the application launches the meeting.

<USER_REQUEST>
# FIRESTORE QUIZ IMPORT SPRINT (PRODUCTION)

Import the quiz dataset into the existing Firestore `quizzes` collection.

## STRICT REQUIREMENTS

* Do NOT modify any question text.
* Do NOT modify any option text.
* Do NOT modify any correct answer.
* Do NOT translate any content.
* Preserve category names exactly as provided.
* Preserve capitalization exactly.
* Preserve punctuation exactly.
* Skip duplicate questions automatically.
* Validate every document before writing.
* Do NOT delete any existing quizzes.
* Do NOT overwrite existing quizzes.
* Only insert new valid quiz documents.

---

# REQUIRED DOCUMENT STRUCTURE

Every document must contain **exactly** these fields:

```json
{
  "question": "...",
  "option1": "...",
  "option2": "...",
  "option3": "...",
  "option4": "...",
  "correctAnswer": "...",
  "category": "..."
}
```

No extra fields.

No missing fields.

---

# VALIDATION RULES

Before inserting every document verify:

✅ question exists

✅ option1 exists

✅ option2 exists

✅ option3 exists

✅ option4 exists

✅ correctAnswer exists

✅ category exists

✅ None of the fields are empty strings

✅ correctAnswer exactly matches one of:

* option1
* option2
* option3
* option4

✅ Category matches exactly as provided.

Do NOT normalize.

Do NOT trim.

Do NOT rename.

---

# DUPLICATE DETECTION

Treat two questions as duplicates when:

question text is identical.

Duplicate questions must:

* NOT be inserted.
* NOT overwrite existing documents.
* Be counted as skipped.

---

# BATCH WRITES

Use Firestore Batch Writes.

Automatically split into multiple batches if necessary to respect Firestore limits (maximum 500 operations per batch).

Commit batches sequentially.

---

# ERROR HANDLING

If one document fails validation:

* Skip only that document.
* Continue importing remaining quizzes.
* Never abort the full import because of one bad record.

If a Firestore batch fails:

* Print the batch number.
* Print the reason.
* Stop further writes to avoid inconsistent imports.

---

# SAFETY

Do NOT modify any other Firestore collection.

Do NOT modify:

* users
* courses
* library
* notifications
* payments
* live_classes
* chats
* status
* analytics

Only operate on the `quizzes` collection.

---

# SUMMARY REPORT

After completion print:

Authentication: SUCCESS / FAILED

Firestore: CONNECTED / FAILED

Documents Processed:

Inserted:

Skipped (Duplicates):

Validation Errors:

Failed Writes:

Batch Count:

Category Counts:

(list every category and total inserted)

Final Total Quiz Count:

Execution Time:

Import Status:

SUCCESS / PARTIAL SUCCESS / FAILED

---

# DATASET

The quiz data starts below.

===============================
